# Skills · L · part 14

865 entries, latent to latex-texstudio. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **latent**: Latent Space — a research concept covering latent, embedding, anchor, subspace, disclosed-approximation. Use when the user's task concerns latent space, or matches: latent, embedding, anchor, subspace, disclosed-approximation, trilateration, sae. Route with loops_route first — this concept's 9 skills and 19 loops are listed in reference/, and a coherent hit often names a different concept's formation. ([mmc7676/Maxey0](https://github.com/mmc7676/Maxey0/tree/HEAD/plugins/maxey0-loops/skills/latent) · 3 copies)
- **latent**: Two-way LLM-native register. User and model communicate by semantic weight - high-signal terms, free structure, explicit confidence - with grammar optional and social or narrative filler dropped. Not compression - length follows meaning, creativity unrestricted. Use when the user invokes /skill:latent or asks for latent mode ("go latent", "latent lite", "latent deep"). Stays on until "stop latent" or "normal mode". ([vitalNohj/latent-bench](https://github.com/vitalNohj/latent-bench/tree/HEAD/skills/latent))
- **latent-anything-review**: Review Python source in this latent-anything repo before commit or merge. Use when the user asks to review, check, verify, or confirm whether changed src code is good, compliant, safe, or ready to commit. Run the real gate with ruff, pyright strict, and pytest, then judge project rules, ADRs, the Rule of Three, and test integrity. Prefer this over generic review for latent-anything source changes. ([triet4p/latent-anything](https://github.com/triet4p/latent-anything/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/latent-anything-review))

- **latent-bug-audit**: Find hidden bugs that work 95% of the time but explode under real conditions. Audits 12 bug classes (network I/O in hot paths, silent data loss, connection leaks, blocking async, unbounded growth, race conditions, SQL injection, etc.). Use before deployments, after incidents, or periodically. ([snqb/my-skills](https://github.com/snqb/my-skills/tree/HEAD/latent-bug-audit) · ★1)
- **latent-bug-investigation**: Investigate existing source code for latent defects that may not have surfaced yet. Use when reviewing Android/iOS/KMP code for crash risks, logic bugs, race conditions, lifecycle leaks, nullability issues, state inconsistencies, edge-case failures, and error-handling gaps before release. ([jtakumi/VtuberCamera_KMP_ver](https://github.com/jtakumi/VtuberCamera_KMP_ver/tree/HEAD/.github/skills/latent-bug-investigation))
- **latent-capability-audit**: **When to use:** Someone reports a missing capability ("there's no override option", "I can't amend this", "there's no way to retry"). Before designing it, prove it does not already exist behind the UI. In a system with a rich domain model, the backend is frequently ahead of the surface — and a feature you *expose* costs a fraction of a feature you *design*. ([briandenicola/online-banking-demo](https://github.com/briandenicola/online-banking-demo/tree/HEAD/.squad/skills/latent-capability-audit) · ★1)

([coachlou/ambient-library](https://github.com/coachlou/ambient-library/tree/HEAD/library/latent-cartographer) · 3 copies)
- **latent-chain-of-thought**: Improve reasoning quality by decoupling reasoning from verbalization. Planner generates deterministic latent trajectories while Decoder grounds them to text. Enables dynamic termination, better Pass@k scaling, and interpretable intermediate states. ([ADu2021/skillXiv](https://github.com/ADu2021/skillXiv/tree/HEAD/skills/skillxiv-v0.0.2-claude-opus-4.6/latent-chain-of-thought) · ★7)
- **latent-chain-of-thought-as-planning**: Decouple reasoning from verbalization using PLaT-inspired latent planning. Maintains a broad solution space through parallel latent trajectories before committing to a single answer. Use when: 'explore multiple solution paths', 'latent reasoning', 'plan before answering', 'search diverse solutions', 'PLaT reasoning', 'breadth-first problem solving'. ([ndpvt-web/arxiv-claude-skills](https://github.com/ndpvt-web/arxiv-claude-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/latent-chain-of-thought-as-planning) · ★15)

- **latent-collaboration-multiagent**: Enable LLM agents to collaborate entirely in latent space by sharing layer-wise KV cache representations instead of text, achieving 4× speedup and 71-84% fewer output tokens while maintaining 14.6% higher accuracy through lossless information preservation in continuous embeddings. ([ADu2021/skillXiv](https://github.com/ADu2021/skillXiv/tree/HEAD/skills/skillxiv-v0.0.2-claude-opus-4.6/latent-collaboration-multiagent) · ★7)

([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/latent-dirichlet-allocation-topic-modeling))

([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/latent-dirichlet-allocation-topic-modeling))
- **latent-embedding-connectivity-assessment**: Use when you have a shared low-dimensional embedding from unpaired multiomic integration (e.g. scConfluence latents in mdata.obsm['latent']) and must quantify alignment quality or hyperparameter sensitivity (e.g. unbalanced-OT m) using purity, FOSCTTM, and graph connectivity.
